Dear friends, I'd like to present to you now 14 urgent requests of physicians on March 14th: 1) Retirement costs must be doubled immediately. 2) The costing system must be abandoned immediately, depending on the performance among physicians, which leads to competition, devalues practice of medicine, and converts patients to points. The wages of public physicians must be arranged in a way that will reflect on retirement, long enough to live as a human being. Doctors who had been forced to resign due to their previous full - time practices were allowed to return to their former duties. I don't think any doctor's MP can say no to these requests, no matter what party they're from, I don't think anyone will say no. 3) The Turkish Penal Code must make changes in order to rid the health environment of violence, says our colleagues. 4) The work of physicians and health workers should be stopped by public opinion and patients, and the functioning of the 184 SABIM Line returning to the doctor's complaint line must be made in line with its purpose. 5) In the public and private sector, physicians should not be given a patient's appointment in less than 20 minutes. 6) The wage inequality between physicians working on the 1st step must be terminated, the rights of institutional physicians must be corrected immediately, and family physicians must be given job security. 7) Any application that restricts the right to free work of physicians must be abandoned. 8) The practice that subcontracts workplace medicine must be stopped immediately. 9) Antidemocratic practices in working life must be immediately removed, especially as mandatory services and temporary assignments that affect the professional, personal, and family life of physicians. 10) The right of all physicians, especially residents, should be applied without exception, not more than fifty-six hours, including 40 hours of work, including seizures. 11) In public health institutions, employees of contracting and subcontractors must be granted the status of civil servants; they must be kept flexible, uncompromising, overworking, and overworking. 12) The economic, administrative and academic autonomy of medical schools hospitals must be maintained. 13) Restrictions in the Health Application Act must be lifted that hinder citizens' health rights and destroy the occupational independence of physicians. 14) The barriers to equal, free health care, which commercialise health care, metamorizing health care, must be removed, and the contributions and participation share must be removed. Thank you so much for listening.

Bribery and corruption are the concepts that people have been working on throughout history. Look, let me tell you about Merton theory. In the United States of America, at the end of the 19th century, at the beginning of the 20th century, several social scientists did serious work on bribery and corruption, and it was called the theory of Merton. Merton and his friend Kay say that in less developed countries, politicians and bureaucrats can't make the legislation right because they're ignorant. If you do not establish the system correctly, if you do not remove the meanings that can be drawn to all sides of the law and if you give the bureaucrat so much authority that it can be done, it can be done, it can be done. Dear friends, in the previous years of the law, when the EPDK first started, there was a concept called "tacking." Someone goes, finds a place to generate energy somewhere, and he gets a license for what he's going to generate energy in his bag. Later, he handed over his license before his firms could travel and produce it, and he wandered through the ministry's bureaucracy. There are serious changes within the existing law to prevent bagging. We've supported all of this in the commission, but unfortunately, as in Merton's concept, in Merton doctrine, there's so much indisputable, so much indisputable stuff here that can be drawn to all sides in the future in the energy market, in the Energy Ministry or in the institution, there's no way to prevent corruption and bribery in the Merton doctrine. First, it is necessary to assume that the applying citizen gives accurate information when giving information and then supervise. The nature of the decree that has been annulled is: Precious lawmakers, Article 128 of the Constitution shall determine the basics and principles of the legal arrangements for public staff. According to the second article of Article 128 of the Constitution: the qualifications, appointments, duties, rights and obligations, monthly allowances and other autonomy work are organised by law. However, collective agreements on financial and social rights are kept. There is a ruling. According to this, regulations for status and autonomy rights must be made by law, but in the first joke of Article 9 of Law 4628, which has been cancelled by the Constitutional Court, the service units of the department; the services of the institution in the form of the number of departments required by the institution's duties and powers; the main services units of service, consulting units and assistance units of the institution, and the organisation of the Council of Ministers shall be governed by a proposal of the Council of Ministers in the first sentence. Thus, the Constitutional Court ruled that the rights of the institutional staff of the EPDK (EPDK) should be regulated by law, while the rights of the institutional staff in the Constitution (EPDK) should be regulated by law, according to the regulation -- which was regulated by regulations at the time -- have been arranged by the Constitution contrary to Articles 7, 123 and 128. The basis for the Constitutional Court's decision is that the regulatory and regulatory institutions, which include the EPDK, are legally established, and must be established in accordance with the principle of governance, as stated in Article 123. In addition, the status of public officials, which have been carried out by regulatory and regulatory institutions and have been conducted according to the basics of public services, must be regulated under Article 128. In the same way, the employment and removal of the staff that the administration will have is also closely related to the status of public officials, which must be regulated by law. Therefore, since the current regulation must be annulled by saying it must cancel the second sentence of the 9th article, its legal gap was proposed to fill this legal gap from May 2012, with the institution's staff, with the institution's structure, the status, the appointment of the institution's personnel, and the rights of identity are also set by this motion. Thus, dear friends, it is now being carried out by the Council of Ministers to move the regulation into the law. Therefore, the motion is in place. I await your support, and salute your high council.
